# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-23)

## Corpus Check
- 5343 files · ~12,224,830 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 260 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 147, (none) 33, .css 16)

## Summary
- 68663 nodes · 199105 edges · 1431 communities (1279 shown, 152 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8469 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `15dd3aaf`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
